question: On what basis the clients are categorized as Partially Approved?
answer: Clients are categorized as Partially Approved based on their ability to deposit and trade before providing the required KYC (Know Your Customer) documentation to the Company for their account to be Approved. Additionally, only Individual and Joint Accounts are eligible to be categorized as Partially Approved; PAMM Accounts, Copy Trading Strategy Providers, and Partners are not allowed to be categorized as such [Account opening agreement 2(1).txt#66@0e779d229c5144c2ac379445b3566afa].

question: Is my telephone call recorded?
answer: Yes, your telephone call may be recorded. The content of any telephone call between you and the company can be recorded and saved as a magnetic or electronic record. The company has the right to use these telephone records as it deems necessary, including in instances of disputes [Account opening agreement(1).txt#103@db975ecc10394c498d9fce5c4b8cab12].

question: What are the limitations on withdrawals?
answer: The limitations on withdrawals include the following conditions:

1. The minimum standard withdrawal amount is ten (10) USD. If the client's balance is less than ten (10) USD, they can choose to "Unfollow" the Strategy and withdraw the entire balance to their Account [Copy trading TC(1).txt#109@68336238a917439b8c3a0db802e35ec9].

2. For withdrawals from Cent, Premium, Raw, Pro, and Pro Plus accounts, the following conditions must be met:
   - Withdrawal requests must not cause the margin level of a Trading Account to fall below 150% during weekdays.
   - Withdrawal requests must not cause the margin level of a Trading Account to fall below 300% during weekends and volatile times [Account opening agreement(1).txt#109@db975ecc10394c498d9fce5c4b8cab12].

3. If a withdrawal request does not meet the requirements specified, it will not be accepted [Account opening agreement(1).txt#110@db975ecc10394c498d9fce5c4b8cab12].

question: What is a leverage and how can i change it?
answer: Leverage is a financial tool that allows a client to control a larger position in the market with a smaller amount of capital. It essentially amplifies both potential gains and potential losses.

You can change the leverage of each trading account in your Client Area. According to the context provided, you can adjust the leverage up to five times without any additional request. If you wish to change it more than five times, you need to send a request to tradingdesk@hfm.com. For demo accounts, you can change the leverage as many times as you want. Additionally, there are no restrictions on increasing the leverage, but you can only decrease it under certain conditions, such as if the margin is zero or if the margin level exceeds 100% [Account opening agreement(1).txt#131@db975ecc10394c498d9fce5c4b8cab12][Client agreement(1).txt#158@95e450ef44aa49f980a3fe0a51cd2e2e].

question: What is the purpose of Analytical cookies?
answer: The purpose of Analytical cookies is to distinguish unique users and pages that users have seen, as well as to count the number of visits to a page or post. They help in analyzing user behavior on the website to improve the user experience and track conversions [1][47].

question: How does high leverage affect trading risk?
answer: High leverage allows traders to hold positions much larger than their actual account value, which can magnify both losses and profits. This means that while high leverage can increase potential gains, it also significantly increases the risk of substantial losses, potentially exceeding the initial margin funds and any additional deposits made to maintain open positions. If the market trend goes against the trader, they may sustain a total loss of their margin funds [Risk disclaimer (1).txt#6@a980eeae471d4efe8b3b92e8bd478bd5][Trading and Execution .txt#24@0b2d6d6b81274968950b05ecb72f95c4].

question: Are CFD transactions with HFM executed on a recognised exchange?

Golden Set ID: 35
question: Does placing a Stop Loss always guarantee that my loss will be limited to the price I selected?
answer: No, placing a Stop Loss does not guarantee that your loss will be limited to the price you selected. Once the Stop Loss order is triggered, it turns into a market order, which may be filled at a price that is worse than the specified stop price. Additionally, market conditions or technological limitations may prevent the order from being executed at the desired price, potentially resulting in larger losses than expected [Risk with CFDS 2.txt#42@6d5ed3fabfc740f0af7c78e3a06][General risk disclosure(1).txt#35@e2d37ef877634605a9f433ae10739688].

question: My Stop Loss was set at a specific price, but major news caused the market to gap through that level. Does HFM have to execute my Stop Loss exactly at the requested price?
answer: No, HFM does not have to execute your Stop Loss exactly at the requested price. Market conditions or technological limitations may make it impossible to execute such orders at the declared price. Once a Stop Loss order is triggered, it turns into a market order, which may be filled at the next best price, potentially resulting in a worse execution than the stipulated price. This means that realized losses can be larger than expected due to market movements, such as gaps caused by major news events [Risk with CFDS 2.txt#42@6d5ed3fabfc740f0af7c78e3a06][General risk disclosure(1).txt#35@e2d37ef877634605a9f433ae10739688].

question: A Copy Trading follower has 400 USD equity, the Strategy Provider has 5000 USD equity and the follower selects 100 percent Volume Allocation. What is the Copy Ratio?
answer: The Copy Ratio is calculated as the equity ratio between the Follower and the Strategy Provider multiplied by the chosen Follower Volume Allocation. 

In this case, the calculation would be:

Copy Ratio = (Follower Equity / Strategy Provider Equity) x Volume Allocation
Copy Ratio = (400 / 5000) x 100/100 = 0.08

Therefore, the Copy Ratio is 0.08. [Copy trading TC(1).txt#122@68336238a917439b8c3a0db802e35ec9]

question: A Strategy Provider has 5000 USD equity. A follower has 400 USD equity and selects 50 percent Volume Allocation. The Strategy Provider opens 0.10 lot. What is the theoretical follower position and what actually happens?
answer: The theoretical follower position size can be calculated as follows:

1. Calculate the trade copy ratio:
   \[
   \text{Trade Copy Ratio} = \frac{\text{Follower Equity}}{\text{Strategy Provider Equity}} \times \frac{\text{Volume Allocation}}{100} = \frac{400}{5000} \times \frac{50}{100} = 0.04
   \]

2. Calculate the follower position size:
   \[
   \text{Follower Position Size} = \text{Strategy Provider Position Size} \times \text{Trade Copy Ratio} = 0.10 \times 0.04 = 0.004 \text{ lots}
   \]

However, the minimum trade size is subject to the follower's approval and confirmation, and it states that the trade will open with a minimum trade size of 0.01 lot. Therefore, since 0.004 lots is below the minimum trade size, the trade will not be copied unless the follower approves a minimum trade size of 0.01 lot.

In summary, the theoretical follower position is 0.004 lots, but practically, the trade will not be copied unless the follower approves a minimum trade size of 0.01 lot [Copy trading TC(1).txt#123@68336238a917439b8c3a0db802e35ec9][Copy trading TC(1).txt#124@68336238a917439b8c3a0db802e35ec9].

question: A Copy Trading follower has 1000 USD in profits and 500 USD in losses. The Strategy Provider charges a 25 percent Performance Fee. How much Performance Fee is payable?
answer: The Performance Fee payable by the Follower is calculated as follows:

Profit = 1000 USD  
Loss = 500 USD  
Performance Fee = 25%

The calculation is:  
(1000 - 500) * 25% = 125 USD

Therefore, the Performance Fee payable is 125 USD. [Copy trading TC(1).txt#128@68336238a917439b8c3a0db802e35ec9]

question: I use an Expert Advisor during a major news event. My EA sends an order at one price but it executes at a very different price. Since HFM allows EAs, must HFM guarantee the EA's requested execution price?
answer: No, HFM does not guarantee the EA's requested execution price. The company allows trading via Expert Advisors but does not offer support regarding their installation, implementation, and proper functioning.
